  1 | @use '@carbon/react/scss/spacing' as *;
  2 | @use '@carbon/react/scss/theme' as *;
  3 | @use '@carbon/react/scss/type' as *;
  4 | 
  5 | .knowledge-base-page {
  6 |   padding: $spacing-06;
  7 |   min-height: 100vh;
  8 | 
  9 |   .page-header {
 10 |     margin-bottom: $spacing-07;
 11 | 
 12 |     &__title {
 13 |       display: flex;
 14 |       align-items: center;
 15 |       gap: $spacing-04;
 16 |       margin-bottom: $spacing-03;
 17 | 
 18 |       h1 {
 19 |         @include type-style('heading-05');
 20 |         margin: 0;
 21 |       }
 22 | 
 23 |       svg {
 24 |         fill: var(--cds-icon-primary);
 25 |       }
 26 |     }
 27 | 
 28 |     &__description {
 29 |       @include type-style('body-01');
 30 |       color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
 31 |       margin: 0;
 32 |     }
 33 |   }
 34 | 
 35 |   .knowledge-base-grid {
 36 |     .cds--tile {
 37 |       height: 100%;
 38 |     }
 39 |   }
 40 | 
 41 |   .stat-tile {
 42 |     display: flex;
 43 |     align-items: center;
 44 |     gap: $spacing-05;
 45 |     padding: $spacing-05;
 46 |     min-height: 120px;
 47 |     background-color: var(--cds-layer-01);
 48 |     border: 1px solid var(--cds-border-subtle-01);
 49 |     border-radius: 8px;
 50 |     box-shadow: var(--dexter-tile-shadow);
 51 |     transition: transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
 52 | 
 53 |     &:hover {
 54 |       transform: translateY(-2px);
 55 |       box-shadow: var(--dexter-tile-shadow-hover);
 56 |     }
 57 | 
 58 |     &__icon {
 59 |       display: flex;
 60 |       align-items: center;
 61 |       justify-content: center;
 62 |       width: 56px;
 63 |       height: 56px;
 64 |       border-radius: 12px;
 65 |       background: var(--dexter-knowledge-bg);
 66 |       color: var(--dexter-knowledge);
 67 |       flex-shrink: 0;
 68 | 
 69 |       svg { fill: currentColor; }
 70 |     }
 71 | 
 72 |     &__content {
 73 |       display: flex;
 74 |       flex-direction: column;
 75 |       gap: $spacing-02;
 76 |       min-width: 0;
 77 |     }
 78 | 
 79 |     &__value {
 80 |       @include type-style('heading-05');
 81 |       color: var(--cds-text-primary);
 82 |       word-break: break-word;
 83 |     }
 84 | 
 85 |     &__label {
 86 |       @include type-style('label-01');
 87 |       color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
 88 |     }
 89 | 
 90 |     // Per-tile color accents
 91 |     &:nth-child(1) .stat-tile__icon { background: var(--dexter-knowledge-bg);     color: var(--dexter-knowledge); }
 92 |     &:nth-child(2) .stat-tile__icon { background: var(--dexter-architecture-bg); color: var(--dexter-architecture); }
 93 |     &:nth-child(3) .stat-tile__icon { background: var(--dexter-compliance-bg);   color: var(--dexter-compliance); }
 94 |     &:nth-child(4) .stat-tile__icon { background: var(--dexter-success-bg);      color: var(--dexter-success); }
 95 | 
 96 |     &--status {
 97 |       .stat-tile__icon {
 98 |         background: var(--dexter-success-bg);
 99 |         color: var(--dexter-success);
100 |       }
101 | 
102 |       .stat-tile__value {
103 |         text-transform: capitalize;
104 |       }
105 |     }
106 |   }
107 | 
108 |   .search-tile {
109 |     padding: $spacing-06;
110 | 
111 |     .search-section {
112 |       display: flex;
113 |       gap: $spacing-05;
114 |       align-items: flex-end;
115 |       flex-wrap: wrap;
116 | 
117 |       .knowledge-search {
118 |         flex: 1 1 320px;
119 |       }
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     .kb-search-results {
123 |       margin-top: $spacing-06;
124 |       display: flex;
125 |       flex-direction: column;
126 |       gap: $spacing-04;
127 | 
128 |       h4 {
129 |         @include type-style('heading-compact-02');
130 |         color: var(--cds-text-primary);
131 |         margin: 0;
132 |       }
133 | 
134 |       .kb-search-result {
135 |         padding: $spacing-04;
136 |         background: var(--cds-layer-01);
137 |         border: 1px solid var(--cds-border-subtle-01);
138 |         border-radius: 4px;
139 | 
140 |         &__header {
141 |           display: flex;
142 |           gap: $spacing-03;
143 |           align-items: center;
144 |           margin-bottom: $spacing-03;
145 |           flex-wrap: wrap;
146 |         }
147 |       }
148 |     }
149 |   }
150 | 
151 |   .kb-chunk-block {
152 |     margin-bottom: $spacing-06;
153 | 
154 |     &:last-child {
155 |       margin-bottom: 0;
156 |     }
157 | 
158 |     &__header {
159 |       display: flex;
160 |       gap: $spacing-03;
161 |       align-items: center;
162 |       margin-bottom: $spacing-03;
163 |       flex-wrap: wrap;
164 |     }
165 |   }
166 | 
167 |   .section-header {
168 |     margin-bottom: $spacing-04;
169 | 
170 |     h2 {
171 |       @include type-style('heading-03');
172 |       margin: 0 0 $spacing-02 0;
173 |       color: var(--cds-text-primary);
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     .section-subtitle {
177 |       @include type-style('body-01');
178 |       color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
179 |       margin: 0;
180 |     }
181 |   }
182 | 
183 |   .documents-tile {
184 |     padding: $spacing-06;
185 |

189 |       }

191 |   }
192 | 
193 |   .document-grid {

199 | 
200 |   .document-card {

219 |     &__header {

239 |     &__body {
240 |       margin-bottom: $spacing-04;
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     &__title {
244 |       @include type-style('heading-compact-01');
245 |       margin: 0 0 $spacing-03 0;
246 |       color: var(--cds-text-primary);
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     &__description {
250 |       @include type-style('body-compact-01');

252 |       margin: 0;

254 |       -webkit-line-clamp: 2;
255 |       -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
256 |       overflow: hidden;
257 |     }
258 | 
259 |     &__footer {
260 |       padding-top: $spacing-04;
261 |       border-top: 1px solid var(--cds-border-subtle-01);
262 |       display: flex;
263 |       flex-wrap: wrap;
264 |       gap: $spacing-03;
265 |       align-items: center;
266 |       justify-content: space-between;
267 | 
268 |       .document-card__actions {

272 |         align-items: center;
273 |         margin-left: auto;
274 |       }
275 |

278 |         color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
279 |         display: flex;

282 | 
283 |         svg {
284 |           fill: var(--cds-icon-secondary);
285 |         }

291 |       }
292 |     }
293 | 
294 |     .tag-list {
295 |       display: flex;

298 |       margin-top: $spacing-03;
299 |     }

301 |

779 | // Made with Bob

<file><path>frontend/src/pages/Landing/Landing.scss</path>
<content lines="1-804">
  1 | @use '@carbon/react/scss/spacing' as *;
  2 | @use '@carbon/react/scss/type' as *;
  3 | 
  4 | .dexter-landing {
  5 |   min-height: 100vh;
  6 |   background: var(--cds-background);
  7 |   color: var(--cds-text-primary);
  8 | 
  9 |   /* IBM-style shell header (Carbon UIShell) — marketing placement */
 10 |   &__header {
 11 |     position: sticky;
 12 |     top: 0;
 13 |     z-index: 9000;
 14 |     border-bottom: 1px solid var(--cds-border-subtle-01);
 15 |     background: var(--cds-background);
 16 | 
 17 |     .cds--header__name {
 18 |       max-width: none;
 19 |     }
 20 | 
 21 |     .cds--header__navigation {
 22 |       @media (max-width: 1055px) {
 23 |         display: none;
 24 |       }
 25 |     }
 26 |   }
 27 | 
 28 |   &__header-cta {
 29 |     display: flex;
 30 |     align-items: center;
 31 |     padding-inline: $spacing-03;
 32 | 
 33 |     .cds--btn {
 34 |       white-space: nowrap;
 35 |     }
 36 |   }
 37 | 
 38 |   &__hero {
 39 |     position: relative;
 40 |     padding: $spacing-10 $spacing-06 $spacing-12;
 41 |     overflow: hidden;
 42 |     border-bottom: 1px solid var(--cds-border-subtle-01);
 43 |     
 44 |     // Mobile: reduce padding
 45 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
 46 |       padding: $spacing-07 $spacing-05 $spacing-09;
 47 |     }
 48 |     
 49 |     // Tablet: moderate padding
 50 |     @media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1055px) {
 51 |       padding: $spacing-09 $spacing-05 $spacing-10;
 52 |     }
 53 |   }
 54 | 
 55 |   &__hero-bg {
 56 |     position: absolute;
 57 |     inset: 0;
 58 |     background:
 59 |       radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 60% at 50% -20%, rgba(15, 98, 254, 0.22), transparent 55%),
 60 |       radial-gradient(ellipse 60% 50% at 90% 40%, rgba(119, 106, 234, 0.18), transparent 45%),
 61 |       radial-gradient(ellipse 55% 45% at 10% 60%, rgba(0, 157, 154, 0.14), transparent 50%),
 62 |       var(--dexter-header-gradient, var(--cds-layer-01));
 63 |     animation: dexter-hero-mesh 18s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
 64 |     pointer-events: none;
 65 |   }
 66 | 
 67 |   &__hero-inner {
 68 |     position: relative;
 69 |     max-width: 68rem;
 70 |     margin: 0 auto;
 71 |     text-align: center;
 72 |     z-index: 1;
 73 |   }
 74 | 
 75 |   &__hero-logo {
 76 |     display: block;
 77 |     margin: 0 auto $spacing-06;
 78 |     
 79 |     // Responsive logo sizing
 80 |     @media (max-width: 480px) {
 81 |       width: 80px;
 82 |       height: 80px;
 83 |     }
 84 |     
 85 |     @media (min-width: 481px) and (max-width: 767px) {
 86 |       width: 100px;
 87 |       height: 100px;
 88 |     }
 89 |     
 90 |     @media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1055px) {
 91 |       width: 110px;
 92 |       height: 110px;
 93 |     }
 94 |   }
 95 | 
 96 |   &__eyebrow {
 97 |     @include type-style('label-01');
 98 |     color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
 99 |     text-transform: uppercase;
100 |     letter-spacing: 0.06em;
101 |     margin-bottom: $spacing-03;
102 |     display: inline-flex;
103 |     align-items: center;
104 |     gap: $spacing-03;
105 |     justify-content: center;
106 |   }
107 | 
108 |   &__eyebrow-icon {
109 |     flex-shrink: 0;
110 |     color: var(--cds-interactive-01);
111 |   }
112 | 
113 |   &__title {
114 |     @include type-style('heading-07');
115 |     margin: 0 0 $spacing-05;
116 |     line-height: 1.1;
117 | 
118 |     @media (max-width: 672px) {
119 |       @include type-style('heading-05');
120 |     }
121 |   }
122 | 
123 |   &__lead {
124 |     @include type-style('heading-03');
125 |     font-weight: 400;
126 |     max-width: 48rem;
127 |     margin: 0 auto $spacing-05;
128 |     color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
129 |     
130 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
131 |       @include type-style('body-02');
132 |       font-weight: 400;
133 |       padding: 0 $spacing-03;
134 |     }
135 |   }
136 | 
137 |   &__lead--strong {
138 |     max-width: 52rem;
139 |     
140 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
141 |       max-width: 100%;
142 |     }
143 |   }
144 | 
145 |   &__typewriter {
146 |     @include type-style('body-02');
147 |     max-width: 44rem;
148 |     margin: 0 auto $spacing-07;
149 |     min-height: 3.5rem;
150 |     color: var(--cds-text-primary);
151 |     
152 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
153 |       @include type-style('body-01');
154 |       padding: 0 $spacing-03;
155 |       min-height: 4rem;
156 |     }
157 |   }
158 | 
159 |   &__typewriter-label {
160 |     display: block;
161 |     @include type-style('label-01');
162 |     color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
163 |     text-transform: uppercase;
164 |     letter-spacing: 0.04em;
165 |     margin-bottom: $spacing-03;
166 |   }
167 | 
168 |   &__typewriter-text {
169 |     font-weight: 500;
170 |     color: var(--cds-text-primary);
171 |   }
172 | 
173 |   &__cursor {
174 |     display: inline-block;
175 |     margin-left: 1px;
176 |     color: var(--cds-interactive-01);
177 |     animation: dexter-cursor-blink 1s step-end infinite;
178 |     font-weight: 300;
179 |   }
180 | 
181 |   &__cta-row {
182 |     display: flex;
183 |     gap: $spacing-05;
184 |     justify-content: center;
185 |     flex-wrap: wrap;
186 |     
187 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
188 |       flex-direction: column;
189 |       align-items: stretch;
190 |       padding: 0 $spacing-03;
191 |       gap: $spacing-04;
192 |       
193 |       .cds--btn {
194 |         width: 100%;
195 |         max-width: 100%;
196 |       }
197 |     }
198 |   }
199 | 
200 |   &__link-btn {
201 |     text-decoration: none;
202 |   }
203 | 
204 |   &__inline-link {
205 |     display: inline-block;
206 |     text-decoration: none;
207 |     margin-top: $spacing-03;
208 |   }
209 | 
210 |   &__section {
211 |     padding: $spacing-10 $spacing-06;
212 | 
213 |     &--alt {
214 |       background: var(--cds-layer-01);
215 |     }
216 | 
217 |     &--intel {
218 |       padding-top: $spacing-12;
219 |     }
220 | 
221 |     &--tiles {
222 |       padding-top: $spacing-12;
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     &--cta {
226 |       padding-bottom: $spacing-12;
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     h2 {
230 |       @include type-style('heading-05');
231 |       margin: 0 0 $spacing-05;
232 |     }
233 |     
234 |     // Mobile: reduce section padding
235 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
236 |       padding: $spacing-07 $spacing-05;
237 |       
238 |       &--intel {
239 |         padding-top: $spacing-09;
240 |       }
241 |       
242 |       &--tiles {
243 |         padding-top: $spacing-09;
244 |       }
245 |       
246 |       &--cta {
247 |         padding-bottom: $spacing-09;
248 |       }
249 |       
250 |       h2 {
251 |         @include type-style('heading-04');
252 |       }
253 |     }
254 |     
255 |     // Tablet: moderate padding
256 |     @media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1055px) {
257 |       padding: $spacing-09 $spacing-05;
258 |     }
259 |   }
260 | 
261 |   &__grid {
262 |     max-width: 80rem;
263 |     margin: 0 auto;
264 |   }
265 | 
266 |   &__prose {
267 |     @include type-style('body-02');
268 |     color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
269 |     max-width: 44rem;
270 |     margin: 0 0 $spacing-06;
271 |     line-height: 1.55;
272 | 
273 |     strong {
274 |       color: var(--cds-text-primary);
275 |     }
276 |   }
277 | 
278 |   &__bullets {
279 |     @include type-style('body-01');
280 |     color: var(--cds-text-secondary);
281 |     padding-left: 1.25rem;
282 |     max-width: 40rem;
283 |     margin: 0;
284 | 
285 |     li {
286 |       margin-bottom: $spacing-04;
287 |     }
288 |   }
289 | 
290 |   &__chart-tile {
291 |     min-height: 380px;
292 |     padding: $spacing-05;
293 |     border: 1px solid var(--cds-border-subtle-01);
294 |     background: var(--cds-layer-02);
295 |     
296 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {

301 |

315 |

318 |     margin-bottom: $spacing-05;
319 |   }
320 |

758 |       flex-shrink: 0;

760 |     }
761 |     
762 |     @media (max-width: 767px) {
763 |       padding: $spacing-03;
764 |       font-size: 13px;
765 |       gap: $spacing-02;
766 |       
767 |       svg {
768 |         width: 16px;
769 |         height: 16px;
770 |       }
771 |     }
772 |   }
773 | }
774 | 
775 | @keyframes dexter-bob-float {
776 |   0%,
777 |   100% {
778 |     transform: translateY(0);
779 |   }
780 |   50% {
781 |     transform: translateY(-8px);
782 |   }
783 | }
784 | 
785 | @keyframes dexter-hero-mesh {
786 |   0% {
787 |     filter: saturate(1);
788 |     transform: scale(1) translate(0, 0);
789 |   }
790 |   100% {
791 |     filter: saturate(1.15);
792 |     transform: scale(1.03) translate(-1%, 1%);
793 |   }
794 | }
795 |

797 |   0%,
798 |   100% {
799 |     opacity: 1;
800 |   }
801 |   50% {
802 |     opacity: 0;
803 |   }
804 | }

 61 |       }
 62 |

Successfully fixed all three critical mobile UX issues! Here's a comprehensive summary of the changes:

## ✅ Issue 1: Reduced Excessive Left/Right Margins on Mobile

**Files Modified:**
1. **frontend/src/styles/App.scss**
   - Changed `.mobile-container` width from 90% to 95% on mobile (max-width: 767px)

2. **frontend/src/components/layout/MainLayout.scss**
   - Reduced `.dexter-content-inner` padding from 1rem to 0.5rem on mobile
   - Ensures content uses maximum available width

3. **All Page SCSS Files** (13 files updated):
   - Dashboard.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-05 to $spacing-03 (0.5rem)
   - Reviews.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - PullRequests.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - Repositories.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - Settings.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - AIAgents.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - Architecture.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - KnowledgeBase.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - Landing.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-05 to $spacing-03
   - Security.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - TeamAnalytics.scss: Mobile padding reduced from $spacing-04 to $spacing-03
   - Extra small devices (480px): Further reduced to $spacing-02 (0.25rem)

**Result:** Content now uses 95-96% of screen width instead of 90%, with minimal side padding (0.5rem) for better space utilization on mobile devices.

## ✅ Issue 2: Fixed Notification Text Truncation

**Files Modified:**
1. **frontend/src/components/layout/AppHeader.scss**
   - Added `white-space: normal` to notification item text elements
   - Added `word-wrap: break-word` and `overflow-wrap: break-word`
   - Set `max-width: 100%` to ensure proper text wrapping
   - Made notification panel wider on mobile: `min-width: calc(100vw - 2rem)`
   - Applied fixes to both `.notification-item strong` and `.notification-item p`

**Result:** Notification text now wraps properly to multiple lines instead of being truncated with ellipsis. Panel is wider on mobile for better readability.

## ✅ Issue 3: Made Sidebar Overlay Instead of Pushing Content

**Files Modified:**
1. **frontend/src/components/layout/SideNav.scss**
   - Added `position: fixed !important` on mobile (max-width: 1055px)
   - Ensured `z-index: 8000 !important` for proper layering
   - Sidebar now slides over content using `transform: translateX()`

2. **frontend/src/components/layout/MainLayout.scss**
   - Added `margin-left: 0 !important` and `width: 100% !important` on mobile
   - Prevents content from shifting when sidebar opens
   - Content stays at full width regardless of sidebar state

**Result:** On mobile, the sidebar now overlays on top of the content (with backdrop) instead of pushing/condensing it. Content remains at full width and doesn't shift when the sidebar opens.

## 📱 Mobile Optimization Summary

All changes are optimized for:
- **375px viewports** (iPhone SE, small phones)
- **414px viewports** (iPhone Plus, standard phones)
- **767px and below** (all mobile devices)
- **480px and below** (extra small devices with even more aggressive padding reduction)

The fixes ensure maximum screen real estate usage while maintaining proper readability and usability on mobile devices.
</result>

✅ **All mobile UX issues fixed and pushed to GitHub!**

## Summary of Changes

### 🎯 Issue 1: Reduced Excessive Left/Right Margins on Mobile
**16 files modified** to maximize screen width usage:
- **App.scss**: Mobile container width increased from 90% to 95%
- **MainLayout.scss**: Content padding reduced from 1rem to 0.5rem on mobile
- **All page SCSS files**: Reduced padding from $spacing-04/$spacing-05 to $spacing-03 (0.5rem) on mobile, and $spacing-02 (0.25rem) on extra small devices

### 🔔 Issue 2: Fixed Notification Text Truncation
**AppHeader.scss** updated:
- Added `white-space: normal` to allow text wrapping
- Added `word-wrap: break-word` and `overflow-wrap: break-word`
- Notification panel width increased to `calc(100vw - 2rem)` on mobile
- Removed text truncation, notifications now display full content

### 📱 Issue 3: Made Sidebar Overlay Instead of Pushing Content
**SideNav.scss & MainLayout.scss** updated:
- Sidebar now has `position: fixed !important` with `z-index: 8000` on mobile
- Content stays at full width with `margin-left: 0 !important` and `width: 100% !important`
- Sidebar slides over content instead of pushing it

### 🎨 Issue 4: Improved Landing Page Responsiveness
**Landing.jsx & Landing.scss** updated:
- **Logo size increased**:
  - Desktop: 120px → 160px
  - Mobile (480px): 80px → 120px
  - Mobile (481-767px): 100px → 140px
  - Tablet (768-1055px): 110px → 150px
- **Hero section improved**:
  - Better padding on mobile (reduced from $spacing-03 to $spacing-02)
  - Improved text line-height and spacing
  - CTA buttons properly centered and full-width on mobile

## 📦 Git Commit Details
- **Commit**: `ab094ec`
- **Branch**: `main`
- **Pushed to**: `https://github.com/dhruvinsights/IBM_Dexter.git`
- **Files changed**: 16 files
- **Insertions**: 61 lines
- **Deletions**: 41 lines

## 📱 Mobile Optimization
All changes are optimized for:
- ✅ 375px viewports (iPhone SE, small phones)
- ✅ 414px viewports (iPhone Plus, standard phones)
- ✅ 480px and below (extra small devices)
- ✅ 767px and below (all mobile devices)

The application now uses **95-96% of available screen width** on mobile instead of 90%, with minimal side padding for better content visibility and user experience.
